---
title: WebCodecs Codec Support Table
description: Real-world WebCodecs codec support across browsers and platforms - 1,087 codec strings tested across 7.7M user sessions. Find which codecs work in Chrome, Safari, Firefox, and Edge.
---

This page contains a comprehensive table of **1,087 codec strings** tested with the WebCodecs API across real-world browsers and platforms.

> **About this dataset:** This data comes from 7,655,853 real user sessions of [free.upscaler.video](https://free.upscaler.video) with a total of 2,434,549,264 individual codec string tests. See the [Codec Support Dataset](/datasets/codec-support/) page for methodology, download links, and usage information. 
## Codec Families

**Video Codecs:**
- [AVC (H.264)](#avc-h264) - 342 variants
- [HEVC (H.265)](#hevc-h265) - 84 variants
- [VP8](#vp8) - 1 variants
- [VP9](#vp9) - 210 variants
- [AV1](#av1) - 432 variants

**Audio Codecs:**
- [Audio Codecs](#audio) - 18 formats
